<h2>Save Big on Cheap Vacation Packages, Hotels, Cruises, and Last-Minute Travel Deals!</h2>
<p>Looking to book your dream vacation at a price you can afford? Dunhill Travel can help. We have a number of search tools available to help you peruse all of the best deals on the Internet. Whether you’re looking for a luxurious cruise, an amenity-rich hotel, a complete vacation package, or a last-minute steal, our search platform can give you access to the best possible prices. To get started, all you have to do is choose a location, pick some dates, and prepare yourself for massive savings. Don’t be shocked when you see just how affordable your dreams can be.</p>
<ul class="nav nav-pills nav-fill nav-justify" role="tablist">
<li class="nav-item">
<a aria-controls="hotel-deals-panel" aria-selected="true" class="nav-link active" data-toggle="pill" href="#hotel-deals-panel" id="hotel-deals-tab" role="tab">Hotel Deals</a>
</li>
<li class="nav-item">
<a aria-controls="cruise-packages-panel" aria-selected="false" class="nav-link" data-toggle="pill" href="#cruise-packages-panel" id="cruise-packages-tab" role="tab">Cruise Packages</a>
</li>
<li class="nav-item">
<a aria-controls="vacation-packages-panel" aria-selected="false" class="nav-link" data-toggle="pill" href="#vacation-packages-panel" id="vacation-packages-tab" role="tab">Vacation Packages</a>
</li>
<li class="nav-item">
<a aria-controls="last-minute-panel" aria-selected="false" class="nav-link" data-toggle="pill" href="#last-minute-panel" id="last-minute-tab" role="tab">Last Minute Deals</a>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="tab-content p-3">
<div aria-labelledby="hotel-deals-tab" class="tab-pane fade show active" id="hotel-deals-panel" role="tabpanel">
<p>Hotels are the second most expensive part of any trip—after airfare. In fact, being unable to find affordable accommodations is one major reason why so many dream trips fall flat. Don’t let hefty price tags crush your dreams. With our help, you can browse some of the best hotel deals the Internet has to offer. Check out five-star suites, exotic yurts, or family-style apartments—the choice is yours. You’ll be able to score great prices on some of the most exclusive destinations without blowing your life savings or taking out a second mortgage on your house.</p>
</div>
<div aria-labelledby="cruise-packages-tab" class="tab-pane fade" id="cruise-packages-panel" role="tabpanel">
<p>With sparkling water surrounding you on every side, there’s nothing better than relaxing on the deck of a cruise ship. Before resigning yourself to a life on the land because of high prices, why not try a search with our cruise deals tool? Our site automatically scans the web to find all of the best prices on some of the top cruise lines including Royal Caribbean, Oceania, Carnival, Celebrity, and more. You’ll be able to find a number of affordable options so that you can travel to your dream destination without breaking the bank.</p>
</div>
<div aria-labelledby="vacation-packages-tab" class="tab-pane fade" id="vacation-packages-panel" role="tabpanel">
<p>Vacations are fun, but they’re even better when you don’t have to handle the planning. Book your entire vacation in one swift click by using our price search tool. Our search platform scours the web to curate the best vacation deals available. You’ll find amazing prices for some of the most exotic trips, such as swimming with the dolphins in the Caribbean or climbing peaks in the Rocky Mountains. Packages may include amenities such as flights, hotels, meals, car rentals, and excursions.  The only thing left will be for you to pack your bags and have fun.</p>
</div>
<div aria-labelledby="last-minute-tab" class="tab-pane fade" id="last-minute-panel" role="tabpanel">
<p>Maybe you’re not one to plan. Instead, you go with the flow. Had a tough week at work? Why not take a weekend vacation to alleviate your stress? If this impromptu style of travel sounds enticing to you, you’ll love our last-minute deals. Because hotels, airlines, and other agencies have openings for upcoming dates, they’ll often lower prices at the last second to attract more people. We curate these deals so that you can browse them and see if anything intrigues you. Be spontaneous and enjoy an adventure.</p>
</div>

<script>
        if (typeof getAllIds === 'undefined') {
            getAllIds = function () {
                return {
                    destination: $('.widget-cruises select[name="destination"]').first().find(':selected').attr('data-id'),
                    length: $('.widget-cruises select[name="length"]').first().find(':selected').attr('data-id'),
                    depart: $('.widget-cruises select[name="depart"]').first().find(':selected').attr('data-id'),
                    line: $('.widget-cruises select[name="line"]').first().find(':selected').attr('data-id'),
                    origin: $('.widget-cruises select[name="origin"]').first().find(':selected').attr('data-id')
                };
            };
        }

        if (typeof getServerData === 'undefined') {
            getServerData = function ($item) {
                $('.cover-form').show();
                $.ajax({
                    type: 'GET',
                    data: getAllIds(),
                    url: '/api/v1/widget-data',
                    success: function (data) {
                        updateForm(data, $item);
                    }
                });
            };
        }

        if (typeof updateForm === 'undefined') {
            updateForm = function (data) {
                let items = data.availableDestinations;
                let selected = $('.widget-cruises select[name="destination"]').first().val();

                if (items.length > 0) {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="destination"]').find('option:not([value="any"])').remove();
                }
                for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
                    let s = '';
                    if (items[i].mapped === selected) {
                        s = 'selected="selected"';
                    }
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="destination"]').append('<option data-id="' + items[i].id + '" value="' + items[i].mapped + '"' + s + '>' + items[i].value + '</option>');
                }

                if (selected != 'any') {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="destination"]').addClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .destination-reset').show();
                } else {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="destination"]').removeClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .destination-reset').hide();
                }


                items = data.availableLengths;
                selected = $('.widget-cruises select[name="length"]').first().val();
                if (items.length > 0) {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="length"]').find('option:not([value="0"])').remove();
                }

                const lengthMap = {
                    '0': 'Any Length',
                    '1': '1-2 Nights',
                    '3': '3-6 Nights',
                    '7': '7-9 Nights',
                    '10': '10-14 Nights',
                    '15': '14+ Nights'
                };

                for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
                    let s = '';
                    if (items[i].value == selected) {
                        s = 'selected="selected"';
                    }
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="length"]').append('<option data-id="' + items[i].id + '" value="' + items[i].value + '"' + s + '>' + lengthMap[items[i].value] + '</option>');
                }

                if (selected != '0') {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="length"]').addClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .length-reset').show();
                } else {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="length"]').removeClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .length-reset').hide();
                }


                items = data.availableDeparts;
                selected = $('.widget-cruises select[name="depart"]').first().val();
                if (items.length > 0) {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="depart"]').find('option:not([value="any"])').remove();
                }
                for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
                    let s = '';
                    if (items[i].id === selected) {
                        s = 'selected="selected"';
                    }
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="depart"]').append('<option data-id="' + items[i].id + '" value="' + items[i].id + '"' + s + '>' + items[i].value + '</option>');
                }

                if (selected != 'any') {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="depart"]').addClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .depart-reset').show();
                } else {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="depart"]').removeClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .depart-reset').hide();
                }



                items = data.availableLines;
                selected = $('.widget-cruises select[name="line"]').first().val();
                if (items.length > 0) {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="line"]').find('option:not([value="any"])').remove();
                }
                for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
                    let s = '';
                    if (items[i].mapped === selected) {
                        s = 'selected="selected"';
                    }

                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="line"]').append('<option data-id="' + items[i].id + '" value="' + items[i].mapped + '"' + s + '>' + items[i].value + '</option>');
                }


                if (selected != 'any') {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="line"]').addClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .line-reset').show();
                } else {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="line"]').removeClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .line-reset').hide();
                }

                items = data.availableOrigins;
                selected = $('.widget-cruises select[name="origin"]').first().val();
                if (items.length > 0) {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="origin"]').find('option:not([value="any"])').remove();
                }
                for (let i = 0; i < items.length; i++) {
                    let s = '';
                    if (items[i].mapped === selected) {
                        s = 'selected="selected"';
                    }
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="origin"]').append('<option data-id="' + items[i].id + '" value="' + items[i].mapped + '"' + s + '>' + items[i].value + '</option>');
                }

                if (selected != 'any') {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="origin"]').addClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .origin-reset').show();
                } else {
                    $('.widget-cruises select[name="origin"]').removeClass('selected');
                    $('.widget-cruises .origin-reset').hide();
                }


                $('.cover-form').hide();
            };
        }

        }).on('afterChange', function(slick,currentSlide){ lazies.update(); }).on('breakpoint', function(event, slick, breakpoint){ lazies.update(); }); }
        if ((sel = $('select:not(.plain)')).length) { sel.select2({ theme: "bootstrap4" }); }
        $('form').validatr({ theme:'bootstrap' });
        $('form').find('input[required], select[required], check[required]').on('invalid', function(){ var $top = $('.validatr-message:visible').first().offset().top - 180; $('html,body').stop().animate({scrollTop: $top}, 500, 'swing'); $('.validatr-message').off().click(function(){$(this).remove();}); });
        $('input:placeholder-shown').focus(function(){var old = $(this).attr('placeholder');$(this).prop('placeholder', '').blur(function(){$(this).prop('placeholder', old);});});
    $('.reset-link').on('click', function(e){
    c = e.currentTarget.className.split(/\s+/)[0].split('-')[0];

    $('.widget-cruises select[name="' + c + '"]').val(c == 'length' ? '0' : 'any');
    $('.widget-cruises .' + c + '-reset').hide();
    getServerData();
    });

    $('.widget-cruises select[name="destination"], .widget-cruises select[name="length"], .widget-cruises select[name="depart"], .widget-cruises select[name="line"], .widget-cruises select[name="origin"]').off('change').on('change', function(e){
    $('.widget-cruises select[name="' + $(this).attr("name") + '"]').val($(this).val());
    getServerData();
    });
    
    });
    </script>
<script>
$('document').ready(function() {
    var locationsLookup = new Bloodhound({
        datumTokenizer: function (d) {
            return customTokenizer(d)
        },
        queryTokenizer: Bloodhound.tokenizers.whitespace,
        prefetch: {url: 'https://www.dunhilltraveldeals.com/storage/queries/locations.json'},
        remote: {url: '/api/v1/lookup/locations/%QUERY', wildcard: '%QUERY', ajax: {dataType: 'jsonp'}},
        sufficient: 1
    });

    function customTokenizer(datum) {
        var iataTokens = Bloodhound.tokenizers.whitespace(datum.iata);
        var labelTokens = Bloodhound.tokenizers.whitespace(datum.label);
        var keywordTokens = Bloodhound.tokenizers.whitespace(datum.keywords);

        return iataTokens.concat(' ').concat(labelTokens).concat(' ').concat(keywordTokens);
    }

    var nyc = [
        {id: 54, label: "(NYC) All Airports"},
        {id: 54, label: "(JFK) John F. Kennedy Intl."},
        {id: 54, label: "(EWR) Newark, New Jersey"},
        {id: 54, label: "(LGA) LaGuardia"},
        {id: 54, label: "(ISP) MacArthur, Islip, New York"},
        {id: 54, label: "(SWF) Stewart Intl., Newburgh, New York"}
    ];

    var nycArray = [
        'nyc',
        'new york',
        '(nyc) all airports',
        '(jfk) john f. kennedy intl.',
        '(ewr) newark, new jersey',
        '(isp) macArthur, islip, new york',
        '(swf) stewart Intl., newburgh, new york'
    ];

    var header = function (data) {
        if (nycArray.includes(data.query.toLowerCase())) {
            return 'New York, United States of America';
        }
        return '';
    };

    var addArrows = function (q) {
        if (nycArray.includes(q.toLowerCase())) {
            return '<h4 class="pl-2 pr-1 d-inline">\u2937</h4>';
        }
        return '';
    };

    $('.is-lookup').typeahead('destroy');
    $('.is-lookup').typeahead({
        autoselect: false,
        hint: false,
        highlight: true,
        minLength: 2
    }, {
        limit: 100,
        name: 'locations',
        display: function (location) {
            return location.label;
        },
        source: function (q, sync, async) {
            if (nycArray.includes(q.toLowerCase())) {
                sync(nyc);
                return;
            }
            $(this).typeahead('val', q);
            locationsLookup.search(q, function (suggestions) {
                    sync(suggestions);
                },
                function (suggestions) {
                    async(suggestions);
                });
        },
        templates: {
            notFound: function (location) {
                return '<div class="text-danger p-1">No Matches Found</div>';
            },
            pending: function (location) {
                return '<div class="p-1">Searching...</div>';
            },
            suggestion: function (location) {
                return '<div>' + addArrows(location._query) + location.label + '</div>';
            },
            header: function (data) {
                return '<div class="p-2">' + header(data) + '</div>';
            }
        }
    }).on('typeahead:selected', function (e, o) {
        $('input[name="' + this.name + '_id"]').val(o.id);
        $('input[name="' + this.name + '_iata"]').val(o.label.substring(1, 3));
    }).on('keydown', function (e) {
        if (e.keyCode === 13 || e.keyCode === 9) {
            e.preventDefault();
            $('.tt-suggestion.tt-selectable.tt-cursor:visible').trigger('click'); // covers case where cursor has moved
            $('.tt-suggestion.tt-selectable:visible').first().trigger('click'); // covers case where cursor hasn't moved
            $(this).closest('form').find('.is-lookup').eq(1).focus();
            $(this).closest('form').find('.tt-menu.tt-open').hide();
        }
    }).on('click', function (e) {
        this.select();
    });
});
